(defprotocol mtls13 diffie-hellman
  (defrole client
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr text) (spk cpk akey)
      (request response text) (x rndx) (y expt))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
            (hash "finished"
              (hash "fk"
                (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
                  cr sr)) (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              s spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
            (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr
              sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash
              (cat (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca)))
              (hash "finished"
                (hash "fk"
                  (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
                    cr sr)) (cat cr (exp (gen) x))
                (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk (enc s spk (privk ca))) c
              cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash "finished"
            (hash "fk"
              (hash "c hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr
                sr))
            (cat
              (cat (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca)))
              (hash "finished"
                (hash "fk"
                  (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
                    cr sr)) (cat cr (exp (gen) x))
                (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk (enc s spk (privk ca))) c
              cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash
                (cat (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca)))
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash "fk"
                    (hash "s hs traffic"
                      (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca))) c cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
              (invk cpk)))
          (hash "c hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr
            sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))))))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr text) (spk cpk akey)
      (request response text) (y rndx) (x expt))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
            (hash "finished"
              (hash "fk"
                (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x)))
                  cr sr)) (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              s spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
            (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) cr
              sr))))
      (recv
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash
              (cat (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca)))
              (hash "finished"
                (hash "fk"
                  (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x)))
                    cr sr)) (cat cr (exp (gen) x))
                (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk (enc s spk (privk ca))) c
              cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash "finished"
            (hash "fk"
              (hash "c hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) cr
                sr))
            (cat
              (cat (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca)))
              (hash "finished"
                (hash "fk"
                  (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x)))
                    cr sr)) (cat cr (exp (gen) x))
                (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk (enc s spk (privk ca))) c
              cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash
                (cat (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca)))
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash "fk"
                    (hash "s hs traffic"
                      (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca))) c cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
              (invk cpk)))
          (hash "c hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) cr
            sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x)))))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))))))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false)))))

(defskeleton mtls13
  (vars (cr sr request response text) (spk cpk akey) (s c ca name)
    (x rndx) (y expt))
  (defstrand client 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (x x)
    (y y))
  (non-orig (invk spk))
  (uniq-orig cr)
  (uniq-gen x)
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr text) (spk cpk akey) (s c ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z 5) (p "client" "cr" z cr)
          (p "client" "sr" z sr) (p "client" "spk" z spk)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "client" "cpk" z cpk)
          (p "client" "c" z c) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (p "client" "y" z y) (uniq-at cr z 0)
          (ugen x) (non (invk spk)))
        (exists ((z-0 strd))
          (and (p "server" z-0 5) (p "server" "cr" z-0 cr)
            (p "server" "sr" z-0 sr) (p "server" "spk" z-0 spk)
            (p "server" "s" z-0 s) (p "server" "cpk" z-0 cpk)
            (p "server" "c" z-0 c) (p "server" "ca" z-0 ca)
            (p "server" "x" z-0 x) (p "server" "y" z-0 y))))))
  (traces
    ((send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
            (hash "finished"
              (hash "fk"
                (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
                  cr sr)) (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              s spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
            (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr
              sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash
              (cat (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca)))
              (hash "finished"
                (hash "fk"
                  (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
                    cr sr)) (cat cr (exp (gen) x))
                (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk (enc s spk (privk ca))) c
              cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash "finished"
            (hash "fk"
              (hash "c hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr
                sr))
            (cat
              (cat (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca)))
              (hash "finished"
                (hash "fk"
                  (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
                    cr sr)) (cat cr (exp (gen) x))
                (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk (enc s spk (privk ca))) c
              cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash
                (cat (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca)))
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash "fk"
                    (hash "s hs traffic"
                      (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca))) c cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
              (invk cpk)))
          (hash "c hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr
            sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))))))))
  (label 0)
  (realized)
  (shape)
  (satisfies
    (no (p "server" z-0 (idx 5)) (p "server" "y" z-0 y)
      (p "server" "x" z-0 x) (p "server" "ca" z-0 ca)
      (p "server" "c" z-0 c) (p "server" "cpk" z-0 cpk)
      (p "server" "s" z-0 s) (p "server" "spk" z-0 spk)
      (p "server" "sr" z-0 sr) (p "server" "cr" z-0 cr) (cr cr) (sr sr)
      (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (x x) (y y) (z 0)))
  (maps
    ((0)
      ((cr cr) (sr sr) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (x x)
        (y y) (request request) (response response))))
  (origs (cr (0 0)))
  (ugens (x (0 0))))

(defprotocol mtls13 diffie-hellman
  (defrole client
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr text) (spk cpk akey)
      (request response text) (x rndx) (y expt))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
            (hash "finished"
              (hash "fk"
                (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
                  cr sr)) (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              s spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
            (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr

                (hash "fk"
                  (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
                    cr sr)) (cat cr (exp (gen) x))
                (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk (enc s spk (privk ca))) c
              cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash "finished"
            (hash "fk"
              (hash "c hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr
                sr))
            (cat
              (cat (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca)))
              (hash "finished"
                (hash "fk"
                  (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
                    cr sr)) (cat cr (exp (gen) x))
                (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk (enc s spk (privk ca))) c
              cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash
                (cat (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca)))
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash "fk"
                    (hash "s hs traffic"
                      (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca))) c cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
              (invk cpk)))
          (hash "c hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr
            sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))))))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr text) (spk cpk akey)

    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))

    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))

    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false)))))

(defskeleton mtls13
  (vars (cr request sr response text) (spk cpk akey) (s c ca name)
    (x rndx) (y expt))
  (defstrand client 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (x x)
    (y y))
  (deflistener request)
  (non-orig (invk spk))
  (uniq-orig cr request)
  (uniq-gen x)
  (goals
    (forall ((cr text) (request text) (spk akey) (x rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z 5) (p "" z-0 2) (p "client" "cr" z cr)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (p "client" "spk" z spk)
          (p "client" "request" z request) (p "" "x" z-0 request)
          (non (invk spk)) (ugen x) (uniq-at cr z 0)
          (uniq-at request z 3)) (false))))
  (traces
    ((send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
            (hash "finished"
              (hash "fk"
                (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
                  cr sr)) (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              s spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
            (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr
              sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash
              (cat (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca)))
              (hash "finished"
                (hash "fk"
                  (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
                    cr sr)) (cat cr (exp (gen) x))
                (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk (enc s spk (privk ca))) c
              cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash "finished"
            (hash "fk"
              (hash "c hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr
                sr))
            (cat
              (cat (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca)))
              (hash "finished"
                (hash "fk"
                  (hash "s hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
                    cr sr)) (cat cr (exp (gen) x))
                (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk (enc s spk (privk ca))) c
              cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash
                (cat (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca)))
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash "fk"
                    (hash "s hs traffic"
                      (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca))) c cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
              (invk cpk)))
          (hash "c hs traffic" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr
            sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))))))))

    (uniq-gen y)

  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
